[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Full 8x8 2-D IDCT of a baseline JPEG decoder (ultraembedded/core_jpeg), module
name `jpeg_idct_2d`: the row pass (jpeg_idct_x, OUT_SHIFT=11), a double-buffered
corner-turn (jpeg_idct_transpose with four 32x32 dual-port RAMs), and the column
pass (jpeg_idct_y, OUT_SHIFT=15), composed exactly as in the original decoder
(downstream ready tied high; the row pass accepts no backpressure). Each 1-D
pass is the factorized (AAN-style) algorithm with cosine constants scaled by
4096 (C1..C7 = 4017, 3784, 3406, 2896, 2276, 1567, 799), time-sharing 5
multipliers over an 8-beat schedule. ALL arithmetic is evaluated in 32-bit
signed context with wrap-around; outputs of each pass are arithmetic-shifted
right (11 then 15); the 1/sqrt(2) odd-part scaling is `(x * 181) / 256` where
`*` wraps at 32 bits and `/` is SIGNED division truncating toward zero. This
exact fixed-point behavior is what the golden vectors encode.

Interface (clocked, sync active-high reset `rst`; no backpressure):
  inputs : clk, rst, img_start_i (1-cycle pulse resets the output streamers),
           img_end_i (unused), inport_valid_i, inport_idx_i[2:0],
           inport_data0..3_i[15:0]
  outputs: outport_valid_o, outport_data_o[31:0], outport_idx_o[5:0]

Input protocol (the upstream block serializer's FIXED schedule, reproduced
exactly by the testbench): per row of 8 coefficients c0..c7 (16-bit signed),
8 back-to-back valid beats with idx = 0..7; the four data lanes carry the EVEN
set (c0,c2,c4,c6) on beats idx in {0,2,5,7} and the ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7) on
beats idx in {1,3,4,6}. 8 rows back-to-back form a 64-beat block; between
blocks there are >= 2 idle cycles (valid=0, idx=0, data=0). Pipeline registers
advance every clock regardless of valid — designs must be robust to the gap
pattern exactly as the original is.

Output (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on outport_valid_o; the
k-th streamed value of a block carries outport_idx_o = {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the
column pass emits column-major; the idx label gives the ROW-MAJOR pixel
position) and must match the expected output at that position. The stream of a
block overlaps the next block's input beats (the transpose double-buffers one
full block).

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` contains the
ORIGINAL core_jpeg modules (Apache 2.0), unmodified, under a thin composition
top. It passes all golden vectors (they were generated from it — the original
RTL is the correctness oracle). Study it, then optimize while preserving its
exact behavior. Sibling benchmark: `jpeg_idct_2d_spire` (identical tb.sv +
vectors.dat, bit-exact SpireHDL starting point). The 1-D row pass alone is
also available as the `jpeg_idct_x_*` benchmarks.

NOTE on memories: the cost-evaluation synthesis flow targets a standard-cell
library with no RAM macros, so every behavioral memory in this design (the
transpose buffers, block_out, etc.) is inferred as a register array plus mux
decode REGARDLESS of coding style. Optimizing that decode/mux logic is fair
game; instantiating a hard RAM macro is not possible in this flow.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = {{16{inport_data0_i[15]}}, inport_data0_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

assign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data_i
    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i
    ,input           outport_ready_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          inport_accept_o
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data1_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data2_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data3_o
    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o
);



reg         block_wr_q;
reg         block_rd_q;
reg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;
reg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;

wire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};

wire [31:0] outport_data0_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data1_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data2_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data3_w;

wire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);
wire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);
wire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);
wire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram0
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr0_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data0_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram1
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr1_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data1_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram2
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr2_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data2_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram3
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr3_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data3_w)
);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Input Buffer
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg [1:0] block_ready_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else if (img_start_i)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else
begin
    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;
        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;
    end

    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;
        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;
    end
end

assign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// FSM
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
localparam STATE_W           = 2;
localparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;
localparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;
localparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;

reg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;
reg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;

always @ *
begin
    next_state_r = state_q;

    case (state_q)
    STATE_IDLE:
    begin
        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)
            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;
    end
    STATE_SETUP:
    begin
        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;
    end
    STATE_ACTIVE:
    begin
        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)
            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
    end
    default: ;
    endcase

    if (img_start_i)
        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
end

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;
else
    state_q <= next_state_r;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_IDLE)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_SETUP)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
begin
    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])
    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd2: ;
    3'd3: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd4: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd5: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd6: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd1;
    3'd7: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    endcase
end

assign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);
assign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];
assign outport_data0_o = outport_data0_w;
assign outport_data1_o = outport_data1_w;
assign outport_data2_o = outport_data2_w;
assign outport_data3_o = outport_data3_w;


endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk0_i
    ,input           rst0_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data0_i
    ,input           wr0_i
    ,input           clk1_i
    ,input           rst1_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data1_i
    ,input           wr1_i

    // Outputs
    ,output [ 31:0]  data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  data1_o
);



//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Dual Port RAM
// Mode: Read First
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
/* verilator lint_off MULTIDRIVEN */
reg [31:0]   ram [31:0] /*verilator public*/;
/* verilator lint_on MULTIDRIVEN */

reg [31:0] ram_read0_q;
reg [31:0] ram_read1_q;


// Synchronous write
always @ (posedge clk0_i)
begin
    if (wr0_i)
        ram[addr0_i][31:0] <= data0_i[31:0];

    ram_read0_q <= ram[addr0_i];
end

always @ (posedge clk1_i)
begin
    if (wr1_i)
        ram[addr1_i][31:0] <= data1_i[31:0];

    ram_read1_q <= ram[addr1_i];
end


assign data0_o = ram_read0_q;
assign data1_o = ram_read1_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_y
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Params
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
#(
     parameter OUT_SHIFT        = 15
    ,parameter INPUT_WIDTH      = 32
)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Ports
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);

[ASSISTANT]
Now I have a thorough understanding of the design. Let me first establish a baseline by evaluating the starting point, then create my optimized version.

Key observations:
1. **Two identical 1-D IDCT modules** (x and y) with the same AAN algorithm, differing only in OUT_SHIFT (11 vs 15) and input width (16 vs 32)
2. **Transpose** uses 4 x 32x32 dual-port RAMs (double-buffered), complex FSM with read address sequencing
3. **Output mux**: x-pass uses simple `ptr_q`, y-pass uses a complex `ptr_conv` LUT for column-major to row-major conversion
4. The y-pass `ptr_conv` function is just `{ptr_q[2:0], ptr_q[5:3]}` (bit swap!) - a huge optimization opportunity
5. Division by 256 is just `>>> 8`
6. The `block_out` array with mux decode is expensive

Let me first evaluate the starting point to get a baseline cost:

[ASSISTANT]
Now I understand the baseline: cost ~9.5M, delay ~2222ps, area ~4274. The critical path is in the transpose RAM read address logic.

Let me create my optimized design with these key optimizations:
1. Simplify the transpose RAM (remove unused write port, simplify to single-port read/write)
2. Replace the y-pass `ptr_conv` LUT with `{ptr_q[2:0], ptr_q[5:3]}` (bit swap)
3. Replace `/256` with `>>> 8`
4. Remove unnecessary pipeline registers (mul_q → mul double registration)
5. Simplify the transpose FSM

Let me start with a straightforward optimization pass:
  -> create_file(filename='design.sv', content="module jpeg_idct_2d\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input   input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [15:0]  inport_data0_i\n    ,input outport_data_o\n    ,output [ 5:0]   outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017;\nlocalparam [15:0] C2_16 = 3784;\nlocalparam [15:0] C3_16 = 3406;\nlocalparam [15:0] C4_16 = 2896;\nlocalparam [15:0] C5_16 = 2276;\nlocalparam [15:0] C6_16 = 1567;\nlocalparam [15:0] C7_16 = 799;\n\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = {{16{inport_data0_i[15]}}, inport_data0_i};\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};\n\nreg signed [31:0] i0;\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_a, mul0_b, mul1_a, mul1_b, mul2_a, mul2_b, mul3_a, mul3_b, mul4_a, mul4_b;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    i0 <= 0; mul0_a <= 0; mul0_b <= 0; mul1_a <= 0; mul1_b <= 0;\n    mul2_a <= 0; mul2_b <= 0; mul3_a <= 0; mul3_b <= 0; mul4_a <= 0; mul4_b <= 0;\nend else begin\n    case (inport_idx_i)\n    3'd0: begin\n        i0 <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C2_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C6_16;\n    end\n    3'd1: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C1_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C7_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C5_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C3_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0; mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    3'd2: begin\n        i0 <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;\n    end\n    3'd3: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd4: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd5: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C6_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C2_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0; mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_q, mul1_q, mul2_q, mul3_q, mul4_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    mul0_q <= 0; mul1_q <= 0; mul2_q <= 0; mul3_q <= 0; mul4_q <= 0;\nend else begin\n    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;\n    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;\n    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;\n    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;\n    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] mul0, mul1, mul2, mul3, mul4;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    mul0 <= 0; mul1 <= 0; mul2 <= 0; mul3 <= 0; mul4 <= 0;\nend else begin\n    mul0 <= mul0_q; mul1 <= mul1_q; mul2 <= mul2_q; mul3 <= mul3_q; mul4 <= mul4_q;\nend\n\nreg [2:0] out_stg0_idx_q;\nreg [2:0] out_stg1_idx_q;\nreg [2:0] out_stg2_idx_q;\nreg       out_stg0_valid_q, out_stg1_valid_q, out_stg2_valid_q, out_stg3_valid_q;\nreg [2:0] out_stg3_idx_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= 0; out_stg2_idx_q <= 0;\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= 0; out_stg3_idx_q <= 0;\nend else begin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;  out_stg0_idx_q <= inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q; out_stg1_idx_q <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q; out_stg2_idx_q <= out_stg1_idx_q;\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q; out_stg3_idx_q <= out_stg2_idx_q;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5, o_s6, o_s7, o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    o_s5 <= 0; o_s6 <= 0; o_s7 <= 0; o_t0 <= 0; o_t1 <= 0; o_t2 <= 0;\n    o_t3 <= 0; o_t4 <= 0; o_t5 <= 0; o_t6 <= 0; o_t7 <= 0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 0; o_t5_6 <= 0;\nend else begin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0: begin\n        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1;\n    end\n    3'd1: begin\n        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;\n        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;\n        o_t0 <= mul4;\n    end\n    3'd2: begin\n        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3;\n        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3;\n        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;\n    end\n    3'd3: begin\n        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);\n    end\n    3'd4: begin\n        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1;\n        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;\n    end\n    3'd5: begin\n        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1;\n        o_t4 <= mul4;\n        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;\n        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;\n    end\n    3'd6: begin\n        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;\n        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;\n        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;\n        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;\n    end\n    default: begin\n        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) >>> 8;\n        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) >>> 8;\n    end\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];\nreg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    block_out[0] <= 0; block_out[1] <= 0; block_out[2] <= 0; block_out[3] <= 0;\n    block_out[4] <= 0; block_out[5] <= 0; block_out[6] <= 0; block_out[7] <= 0;\n    block_out_tmp <= 0;\nend else if (out_stg3_valid_q) begin\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3) begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7) begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\nend\n\nreg [7:0] valid_q;\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)       valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i) valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse           valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)       ptr_q <= 0;\nelse if (img_start_i) ptr_q <= 0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o) ptr_q <= ptr_q + 1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\nassign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// 1-D IDCT Y (column pass) — OUT_SHIFT=15, INPUT_WIDTH=32\n// Same algorithm but inputs are already 32-bit\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_y #(\n    parameter OUT_SHIFT = 15,\n    parameter INPUT_WIDTH = 32\n) (\n    input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [31:0]  inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [31:0]  inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [31:0]  inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [31:0]  inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [ 2:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data_o\n    ,output [ 5:0]   outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017;\nlocalparam [15:0] C2_16 = 3784;\nlocalparam [15:0] C3_16 = 3406;\nlocalparam [15:0] C4_16 = 2896;\nlocalparam [15:0] C5_16 = 2276;\nlocalparam [15:0] C6_16 = 1567;\nlocalparam [15:0] C7_16 = 799;\n\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = inport_data0_i;\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = inport_data1_i;\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = inport_data2_i;\nwire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = inport_data3_i;\n\nreg signed [31:0] i0;\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_a, mul0_b, mul1_a, mul1_b, mul2_a, mul2_b, mul3_a, mul3_b, mul4_a, mul4_b;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    i0 <= 0; mul0_a <= 0; mul0_b <= 0; mul1_a <= 0; mul1_b <= 0;\n    mul2_a <= 0; mul2_b <= 0; mul3_a <= 0; mul3_b <= 0; mul4_a <= 0; mul4_b <= 0;\nend else begin\n C3_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0; mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    3'd2: begin\n        i0 <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;\n    end\n    3'd3: begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n mul0_q, mul1_q, mul2_q, mul3_q, mul4_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    mul0_q <= 0; mul1_q <= 0; mul2_q <= 0; mul3_q <= 0; mul4_q <= 0;\nend else begin\n    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;\n    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;\n    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;\n    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;\n    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] mul0, mul1, mul2, mul3, mul4;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    mul0 <= 0; mul1 <= 0; mul2 <= 0; mul3 <= 0; mul4 <= 0;\nend else begin\n    mul0 <= mul0_q; mul1 <= mul1_q; mul2 <= mul2_q; mul3 <= mul3_q; mul4 <= o_s5, o_s6, o_s7, o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    o_s5 <= 0; o_s6 <= 0; o_s7 <= 0; o_t0 <= 0; o_t1 <= o_t3;\n        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;\n        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;\n    end\n    default: begin\n        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) >>> 8;\n        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) >>> 8;\n    end\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];\nreg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    block_out[0] <= 0; block_out[1] <= 0; block_out[2] <= 0; block_out[3] <= 0;\n    block_out[4] <= 0; block_out[5] <= 0; block_out[6] <= 0; block_out[7] <= 0;\n    block_out_tmp <= 0;\nend else if (out_stg3_valid_q) begin\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3) begin\n valid_q;\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)       valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i) valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse           valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst)       ptr_q <= 0;\nelse if (img_start_i) ptr_q <= 0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o) ptr_q <= ptr_q + 1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\nassign outport_idx_o   = {ptr_q[2:0], ptr_q[5:3]};\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Transpose (corner-turn) with double-buffered RAMs\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose (\n    input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [31:0]  inport_data_i\n    ,input  [ 5:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,input           outport_ready_i\n    ,output          inport_accept_o\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [31:0]  outport_data0_o\n    ,output [31:0]  outport_data1_o\n    ,output [31:0]  outport_data2_o\n    ,output [31:0]  outport_data3_o\n    ,output [ 2:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nreg         block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\nreg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;\n\nwire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};\n\nwire [31:0] outport_data0_w;\nwire [31:0] outport_data1_w;\nwire [31:0] outport_data2_w;\nwire [31:0] outport_data3_w;\n\nwire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);\nwire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);\nwire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);\nwire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);\n\njpeg_idct_transpose_ram u_ram0 (\n    .clk(clk), .rst(rst), .wr_addr(wr_ptr_w), .wr_data(inport_data_i), .wr_en(wr0_w),\n    .rd_addr({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q}), .rd_data(outport_data0_w)\n);\njpeg_idct_transpose_ram u_ram1 (\n    .clk(clk), .rst(rst), .wr_addr(wr_ptr_w), .wr_data(inport_data_i), .wr_en(wr1_w),\n    .rd_addr({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q}), .rd_data(outport_data1_w)\n);\njpeg_idct_transpose_ram u_ram2 (\n    .clk(clk), .rst(rst), .wr_addr(wr_ptr_w), .wr_data(inport_data_i), .wr_en(wr2_w),\n    .rd_addr({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q}), .rd_data(outport_data2_w)\n);\njpeg_idct_transpose_ram u_ram3 (\n    .clk(clk), .rst(rst), .wr_addr(wr_ptr_w), .wr_data(inport_data_i), .wr_en(wr3_w),\n    .rd_addr({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q}), .rd_data(outport_data3_w)\n);\n\n// Input Buffer\nreg [1:0] block_ready_q;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) begin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0; block_wr_q <= 1'b0; block_rd_q <= 1'b0;\nend else if (img_start_i) begin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0; block_wr_q <= 1'b0; block_rd_q <= 1'b0;\nend else begin\n    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o) begin\n        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n        block_wr_q <= ~block_wr_q;\n    end\n    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63) begin\n        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;\n        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;\n    end\nend\n\nassign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\n// FSM\nlocalparam STATE_IDLE   = 2'd0;\nlocalparam STATE_SETUP  = 2'd1;\nlocalparam STATE_ACTIVE = 2'd2;\n\nreg [1:0] state_q;\nreg [1:0] next_state_r;\n\nalways @* begin\n    next_state_r = state_q;\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE: if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i) next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;\n    STATE_SETUP: next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;\n    STATE_ACTIVE: if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63) next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\n    default: ;\n    endcase\n    if (img_start_i) next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\nend\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nif (rst) state_q <= STATE_IDLE;\nelse     state_q <= next_state_r;\n\nalways [Step 13/60]
// Self-checking testbench for jpeg_idct_2d — the full 8x8 2-D IDCT
// (x-pass -> transpose -> y-pass) of ultraembedded/core_jpeg.
// Reads vectors.dat: one line per 8x8 block = 64 input coefficients (16-bit
// signed, 4 hex digits, row-major c[row][col]) followed by 64 expected outputs
// (32-bit, 8 hex digits, in ROW-MAJOR pixel order — outputs are matched via
// outport_idx_o, which labels each streamed value with its row-major position).
//
// Drive protocol: identical to the jpeg_idct_x benchmark (the upstream block
// serializer's fixed schedule): per row 8 back-to-back valid beats idx=0..7,
// EVEN coefficient set (c0,c2,c4,c6) at idx in {0,2,5,7}, ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7)
// at idx in {1,3,4,6}; 8 rows back-to-back per 64-beat block; >= 2 idle cycles
// between blocks (valid=0, idx=0, data=0).
//
// Output contract (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on
// outport_valid_o; the k-th value of a block carries
// outport_idx_o == {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the y-pass un-transpose permutation) and
// must equal the expected output at that row-major position. Comparison is
// EXACT (integer outputs).
module tb;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic        clk;
  logic        rst;
  logic        img_start;
  logic        inport_valid;
  logic [15:0] inport_data0, inport_data1, inport_data2, inport_data3;
  logic [2:0]  inport_idx;
  logic        outport_valid;
  logic [31:0] outport_data;
  logic [5:0]  outport_idx;

  jpeg_idct_2d dut (
    .clk(clk), .rst(rst),
    .img_start_i(img_start), .img_end_i(1'b0),
    .inport_valid_i(inport_valid),
    .inport_data0_i(inport_data0), .inport_data1_i(inport_data1),
    .inport_data2_i(inport_data2), .inport_data3_i(inport_data3),
    .inport_idx_i(inport_idx),
    .outport_valid_o(outport_valid),
    .outport_data_o(outport_data),

  logic [15:0] coeffs [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  logic [31:0] expy   [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  int num_blocks;

  task automatic load_vectors;
    int fd, rc, n;
    logic [31:0] tok;
    logic [31:0] flat [0:MAX_BLOCKS*128-1];
    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    n = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      rc = $fscanf(fd, "%h", tok);
      if (rc == 1) begin
        flat[n] = tok;
        n = n + 1;
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);
    num_blocks = n / 128;
    if (num_blocks * 128 != n)
      $display("WARNING: %0d trailing tokens ignored", n - num_blocks * 128);
    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++)
      for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) begin
        coeffs[b*64+i] = flat[b*128+i][15:0];
        expy[b*64+i]   = flat[b*128+64+i];
      end
  endtask

  // ---- output checker -------------------------------------------------------
  int out_cnt;
  bit checking;
  logic [5:0] k;

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    #1;
    if (checking && outport_valid) begin
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      k = out_cnt % 64;
      if (outport_idx !== {k[2:0], k[5:3]}) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d out=%0d idx=%0d expected_idx=%0d",
                 out_cnt / 64, out_cnt % 64, outport_idx, {k[2:0], k[5:3]});
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
      if (outport_data !== expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx]) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d pos=%0d expected_y=%08x actual_y=%08x",
                 out_cnt / 64, outport_idx, expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx],
                 outport_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
`ifdef DUMP_Y
      $display("Y %02x %08x", outport_idx, outport_data);
`endif
      out_cnt = out_cnt + 1;
    end
  end

  // ---- driver ----------------------------------------------------------------
  task automatic drive_beat(input logic valid, input logic [2:0] kk,
                            input logic [15:0] d0, d1, d2, d3);
    @(negedge clk);
    inport_valid = valid;
    inport_idx   = kk;
    inport_data0 = d0;
    inport_data1 = d1;
    inport_data2 = d2;
    inport_data3 = d3;
  endtask

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    out_cnt = 0;
    checking = 0;
    rst = 1;
    img_start = 0;
    inport_valid = 0;
    inport_idx = 0;
    inport_data0 = 0; inport_data1 = 0; inport_data2 = 0; inport_data3 = 0;

    load_vectors();

    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 1;             // one-cycle image-start pulse
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 0;
    checking = 1;

    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++) begin
      for (int r = 0; r < 8; r++) begin
        for (int kb = 0; kb < 8; kb++) begin
          if (kb == 0 || kb == 2 || kb == 5 || kb == 7)
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+4], coeffs[b*64+r*8+6]);
          else
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+1], coeffs[b*64+r*8+3],
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+5], coeffs[b*64+r*8+7]);
        end
      end
      // inter-block gap: >= 2 idle cycles (serializer IDLE+SETUP), varied a bit
      repeat (2 + (b % 3)) drive_beat(1'b0, 3'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0);
    end
    drive_beat(1'b0, 3'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0);
    repeat (400) @(negedge clk);  // flush x + transpose + y pipeline

    if (out_cnt != num_blocks * 64) begin
      $display("TB_ERROR missing outputs: got %0d, expected %0d", out_cnt, num_blocks * 64);
      total_errors = total_errors + 1;
    end
    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

// Input Buffer
reg [1:0] block_ready_q;

always @(posedge clk)
if (rst) begin
    block_ready_q <= 2'b0; block_wr_q <= 1'b0; block_rd_q <= 1'b0;
end else if (img_start_i) begin
    block_ready_q <= 2'b0; block_wr_q <= 1'b0; block_rd_q <= 1'b0;
end else begin

        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;
        block_wr_q <= ~block_wr_q;

        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;
        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;
    end
end

assign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];

localparam STATE_IDLE   = 2'd0;
localparam STATE_SETUP  = 2'd1;
localparam STATE_ACTIVE = 2'd2;

reg [1:0] state_q;
reg [1:0] next_state_r;

always @* begin
    next_state_r = state_q;

    STATE_IDLE: if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i) next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;
    STATE_SETUP: next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;
    STATE_ACTIVE: if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63) next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;

Now let me rewrite the transpose to compute the read address combinationally from rd_idx_q. The key insight: at beat k during ACTIVE, the read address should be `{block_rd_q, addr_func(k)}` where addr_func is derived from my trace. In the original, SETUP state presents addr=8, and ACTIVE starts at the next cycle with the data from addr=8 available. So the address for beat k needs to be presented one cycle BEFORE beat k's data is consumed.

1. Split the 32-entry array into two 16-entry arrays (4-bit address each) — but that doesn't help since it's still a 16:1 mux + another level.
2. Reduce the number of entries by changing the transpose buffer organization.

But wait: in the original, at cycle with out_stg2_idx_q=1, it uses mul0=mul0_q from stage 2 (which was mul0_a*mul0_b from stage 0 at idx 1). But mul0_a at idx 1 was set from block_in_0_1 (the CURRENT input). So mul0_q at stage 1 = block_in_0_1 * C1_16 (computed from input at idx 1). Then mul at stage 2 = same value.

If I remove the double-register: mul at stage 1 = mul0_a * mul0_b. mul0_a was set at stage 0 (idx k), so at stage 1 (idx k+1), mul0 = mul0_a(k) * mul0_b(k). But the output computation at idx k+1 uses mul0... let me check if the indices still align.

In the original:
- Stage 0 (out_stg0_idx_q=k): mul_a/b set from input at idx k
- Stage 1 (out_stg1_idx_q=k): mul_q = mul_a(k) * mul_b(k)
- Stage 2 (out_stg2_idx_q=k): mul = mul_q = mul_a(k) * mul_b(k); output computation uses out_stg2_idx_q=k and mul=mul(k)

The output computation at stage 2 (idx k) uses mul results from stage 0 (idx k). There's a 2-cycle delay. If I remove one stage, it becomes 1-cycle delay. The output at stage 1 (idx k) would use mul from stage 0 (idx k). But mul = mul_a * mul_b registered, so at the rising edge of stage 1, mul = mul_a(k-1) * mul_b(k-1) = results from idx k-1. That's wrong - we need results from idx k, not k-1.

The issue is: the multiplier takes 1 cycle (mul_q = mul_a * mul_b). So mul_a/b at idx k → mul_q at idx k+1. In the original, there's an extra pipeline register (mul = mul_q) making it idx k+2. The output computation at idx k+2 uses mul from idx k. This 2-cycle alignment is essential because some output computations need values from 2 cycles ago (e.g., at idx 1, o_t0 = mul4 which was set at idx 0 via mul4_a=i0, and i0 was set at idx 0 from inputs).

Actually, looking more carefully: at idx 1, mul4_a = i0 (which was computed at idx 0). So mul4_q at idx 2 = i0 * C4. And the output at idx 1 (out_stg2_idx_q=1) uses o_t0 = mul4. But mul4 at stage 2 = mul4_q at stage 1 = mul4_a * mul4_b where mul4_a was set at stage 0 (idx 1). So mul4 at stage 2 = i0 * C4 where i0 was set at idx 0. Actually, the mul_a/b at idx k are set from inputs at idx k. mul_q at stage 1 (idx k+1 in the pipeline) = mul_a(k) * mul_b(k) = results from inputs at idx k. mul at stage 2 (idx k+2) = same. The reason for the extra register: the multiplier takes 1 cycle. At cycle T, mul_a/b are set. At cycle T+1, mul_q = mul_a * mul_b. The output computation at out_stg2_idx_q=k happens at cycle T+2 (where T is when input idx k arrived). So it uses mul = mul_q(T+1) = mul_a(T) * mul_b(T). This works because the output computation needs values from the SAME idx k, and those values are available 2 cycles later.

If I remove the extra register, the output would use mul_q at stage 1 (cycle T+1). But at cycle T+1, the input is at idx k+1, and out_stg1_idx_q would be k (it was set at cycle T). This means my formula for read_addr_at_beat(k) was the value of rd_addr_q at beat k, NOT the actual RAM address being read. The actual address read at beat k is rd_addr_q at beat k-1 (due to the registered read).

[ASSISTANT]
Let me take a completely different approach. Instead of tracing addresses, let me think about what the transpose actually does and implement it from scratch with a simpler structure.

The transpose stores 64 32-bit values (8x8 block) from the x-pass (row-major order), and reads them in column-major order for the y-pass. The double-buffering means two blocks can be stored simultaneously.

The key constraint: the y-pass needs 4 data values per cycle (data0..data3) for 8 beats per column, 8 columns per block = 64 beats total.

The original uses 4 RAMs, each storing 16 values (2 rows × 8 columns per buffer, 32 entries with double buffering). The 4 RAMs correspond to which 2-row group a value belongs to.

Let me think about this differently: can I use a simple 64-entry array (per buffer) with write address = inport_idx_i and read address = transpose(inport_idx_i)? The read would be a 64:1 mux, but with registered read it's one cycle. However, 64 entries → 64:1 mux is worse than 32:1.

Actually, let me try: 2 banks of 32 entries each (selected by block_rd_q). Each bank is 32 entries → 32:1 mux. But with a registered read, the address is presented and data appears next cycle. Since block_rd_q selects the bank, and I can use it as the MSB, this is what we already have.

Let me try yet another approach: can I pipeline the read address? Add an extra register stage for the read address. This would increase latency by 1 cycle but break the critical path. The original already has SETUP for this purpose, so maybe I can add another cycle.

Actually, wait - the critical path is `rd_addr_q QN → inverter → mux → rd_q`. The 1377ps is the QN output delay. This is because Yosys uses a DFFHQNx1 (Q-bar output flop) and the QN path is inherently slow. If I could make the mux use the Q output instead, it would be faster.

The QN is used because the mux decode needs the complement of some address bits. If I restructure the RAM so that the address bits are used in their true form only, Yosys might use a Q-output flop instead.

Let me try inverting the address bits before the RAM, or using a different RAM organization:

[ASSISTANT]
## Summary

### Final Result
- **Best cost:** 8,542,816 area_delay_product (PASS, 100% correctness)
- **Baseline (starting_point.v):** 9,496,575 area_delay_product
- **Improvement:** ~10% reduction

### What Worked Best
The final `design.sv` achieved cost reduction through these key optimizations:
1. **Simplified transpose RAM module** — Replaced the original dual-port RAM (`jpeg_idct_transpose_ram`) with a simpler single-write-port/single-read-port version, removing unused write port 1 logic, the `data0_o` output, and redundant `ram_read0_q` register.
2. **Y-pass output index simplification** — Replaced the 64-entry `ptr_conv` LUT function in `jpeg_idct_y` with a simple bit-swap: `assign outport_idx_o = {ptr_q[2:0], ptr_q[5:3]}`. This eliminated a large 6-bit LUT.
3. **Code cleanup** — Consolidated the 3 separate always blocks for pipeline stage registers into one, and simplified the FSM/wiring while preserving exact cycle-accurate behavior.

### What Didn't Work / Caused Regressions
1. **Combinational read address computation** — Attempted to replace the `rd_addr_q` register (with its complex +8/-8/+1 case statement) with a combinational formula derived from `rd_idx_q`. Spent significant effort deriving the address pattern (`{f(k[2:0]), (k+1)[5:3]}`) but the formula never matched the original's behavior — the registered read latency and FSM timing were too subtle to trace correctly by hand. Failed 3 times with different formulas.
2. **Replacing `/256` with `>>> 8`** — The spec requires SIGNED division truncating toward zero, which differs from arithmetic right shift (floor) for negative numbers. Caused 4 off-by-one errors.
3. **Removing RAM reset** — Removing the `if(rst)` reset from the 32-entry RAM array made things dramatically worse (11.8M cost, 2757ps delay) — Yosys inferred worse flip-flop types without the reset.
4. **Banked RAM (4×8 entries)** — Explicitly splitting the 32-entry RAM into 4 banks of 8 with a case statement made it catastrophically worse (18.7M, 4438ps) — the case-based bank select created worse mux logic than the flat array.
5. **Removing the mul_q→mul pipeline stage** — Considered but rejected because it would put the multiplier and adder in the same combinational cycle, likely increasing delay.

### Key Lessons
- The critical path is `rd_addr_q` QN output (1377ps clock-to-Q) → 32:1 mux → `rd_q` register. The 32-entry register array infers a 32:1 mux that dominates delay, and this is fundamentally hard to optimize in a standard-cell flow without RAM macros.
- The 1377ps QN delay suggests Yosys chose a Q-bar output flop for the address register — restructuring to avoid needing the complement might help, but I couldn't find a working approach.
- `target_delay` had minimal effect on area (4156 vs 4154) — the RTL structure was the dominant factor.
- **Next time:** I would invest more effort in understanding the transpose address pattern — possibly by creating a self-contained testbench (not the provided one) to trace the exact address sequence, then verify the combinational formula before integrating. I would also explore alternative transpose buffer organizations (e.g., 8 RAMs of 16 entries to reduce mux width) while carefully managing the read port selection logic.
